# The VDU drivers' screens: each case run by genuine RISC OS 5's BASIC V on
# the screen, and what it read and drew recorded, which the replay holds the
# drivers to. A case opens `case NAME`; its lines follow, numbered 10 to
# 8999. The frame (`tairix_riscos_vdu::corpus::program`) defines every
# character from 32 to 255 as the first-party font draws it, gives the case
# `PROCmode`, `PROCscreen`, `PROCr`, `FNm`, `FNv` and `FNs`, and saves the
# screen at the end. A case that defines characters of its own defines the
# font again before it ends, since the frame refuses a screen whose
# characters no longer match the font. The cases run one after another in
# one session, as the replay runs them, so what a case leaves that a mode
# change does not reset is the next case's to find.

case mode_variables_every_numbered_mode
10 SYS "OS_ChangeDynamicArea",2,&200000
20 FOR m%=0 TO 53:PROCmode(m%):PROCr("m"+STR$m%,FNm(0))
30 FOR v%=1 TO 12:PROCr(" v"+STR$v%,FNm(v%)):NEXT
40 FOR v%=162 TO 170:IF v%<>166 THEN PROCr(" w"+STR$v%,FNv(v%))
50 NEXT:PROCr(" ecfy",FNz(84)):PROCr(" ecfx",FNz(83))
60 NEXT:PROCmode(25)
70 GOTO 9000
80 DEFFNz(i%):LOCAL a%:SYS "OS_ReadSysInfo",6,0,i% TO ,,a%:=!a%

case lines_every_octant
10 PROCmode(25)
20 RESTORE 50:FOR i%=1 TO 36:READ x%,y%
30 MOVE 640,480:DRAW x%,y%:NEXT
40 GOTO 9000
50 DATA 1140,480,1132,552,1109,623,1073,690,1023,749,961,801
60 DATA 890,843,811,874,726,893,640,900,553,893,468,874
70 DATA 390,843,318,801,256,749,206,690,170,623,147,552
80 DATA 140,480,147,407,170,336,206,270,256,210,318,158
90 DATA 390,116,468,85,553,66,640,60,726,66,811,85
100 DATA 890,116,961,158,1023,210,1073,270,1109,336,1132,407

case lines_short_and_steep
10 PROCmode(25)
20 FOR i%=0 TO 15:MOVE 40+i%*76,40:DRAW 40+i%*76+i%*2,40+i%*24:NEXT
30 FOR i%=0 TO 15:MOVE 40+i%*76,600:DRAW 40+i%*80-i%*2,600-i%*6:NEXT
40 MOVE 100,900:DRAW 100,900:MOVE 200,900:DRAW 203,901

case line_end_rules
10 PROCmode(25)
20 FOR t%=0 TO 7:y%=40+t%*114
30 MOVE 60,y%:PLOT t%*8+5,300,y%+40:MOVE 400,y%:PLOT t%*8+5,410,y%+100
40 MOVE 800,y%+90:PLOT t%*8+5,1000,y%:MOVE 1100,y%:PLOT t%*8+5,1100,y%+8:NEXT

case dotted_lines_and_patterns
10 PROCmode(25)

30 VDU 23,6,&F0,&CC,&AA,&81,&FF,0,&F3,&18:SYS "OS_Byte",163,242,23
40 FOR i%=0 TO 5:MOVE 40,600+i%*50:PLOT 61,600,620+i%*50:PLOT 61,1200,600+i%*50:NEXT
50 SYS "OS_Byte",163,242,0:MOVE 40,920:PLOT 21,1200,920

case points_and_relative_moves

40 MOVE 600,600:PLOT 0,10,10:PLOT 1,40,0:PLOT 1,0,40:PLOT 1,-40,0

case gcol_and_tints_in_256_colours
10 PROCmode(28)
20 FOR c%=0 TO 63:FOR t%=0 TO 3:GCOL 0,c% TINT t%*64
30 RECTANGLE FILL (c% MOD 16)*80+t%*20,(c% DIV 16)*200,19,199:NEXT:NEXT
40 FOR a%=1 TO 7:GCOL a%,21 TINT 128:RECTANGLE FILL a%*150,820,100,120:NEXT
50 PROCr("gfcol",FNv(153)):PROCr("gftint",FNv(157)):PROCr("ora",FNz(0)):PROCr("eor",FNz(4))
60 GOTO 9000
70 DEFFNz(o%):LOCAL a%:SYS "OS_ReadSysInfo",6,0,72 TO ,,a%:=a%!o%

case colour_blocks_in_each_depth
10 FOR m%=0 TO 28:IF m%<>7 AND m%<>3 AND m%<>6 AND m%<>11 AND m%<>14 AND m%<>17 THEN PROCm(m%)
20 NEXT:PROCmode(25):GOTO 9000
30 DEFPROCm(m%):LOCAL a%,k%:PROCmode(m%):GCOL 0,1:GCOL 3,128+2
40 SYS "OS_ReadSysInfo",6,0,72 TO ,,a%:FOR k%=0 TO 3:PROCr("m"+STR$m%+" fg"+STR$k%,a%!(k%*4)):NEXT
50 SYS "OS_ReadSysInfo",6,0,73 TO ,,a%:FOR k%=0 TO 3:PROCr("m"+STR$m%+" bg"+STR$k%,a%!(k%*4)):NEXT
60 ENDPROC

case clears_and_home

case mode_strings

case check_mode_valid
10 FOR m%=0 TO 60 STEP 3:SYS "XOS_CheckModeValid",m% TO r0%,r1%;f%:PROCr("m"+STR$m%,r0%):PROCr(" c",f% AND 2)
20 IF (f% AND 2)<>0 AND r1%<256 THEN PROCr(" alt",r1%)
25 IF (f% AND 2)<>0 AND r1%>255 THEN PROCs(r1%)
30 NEXT:DIM s% 64:s%!0=1:s%!4=800:s%!8=600:s%!12=3:s%!16=-1:s%!20=-1
40 SYS "XOS_CheckModeValid",s% TO r0%,r1%;f%:PROCr("sel",r0%):PROCr("selc",f% AND 2):PROCr("selalt",r1%)
50 GOTO 9000
60 DEFPROCs(a%):LOCAL k%:FOR k%=0 TO 9:PROCr(" alt"+STR$k%,a%!(k%*4)):NEXT:ENDPROC

250 PROCp("pq","q")
260 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"n1",0,3,2,&181680B5 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("n1",FNe(r0%,f%))

300 PROCf("n2",9)
310 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+35,A%,"n1","n2",1 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("n12 below",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("n12 below",A%)
320 PROCp("n12","n1")
330 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"w1",0,7,2,12 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("w1",FNe(r0%,f%))
340 o%=FNo:A%!(o%+24)=8
350 PROCf("w1",1)
360 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"w2",0,2,2,12 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("w2",FNe(r0%,f%))

410 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+37,A%,"pa",1
420 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"pb",0,2,2,12 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("pal2",FNe(r0%,f%))
430 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+35,A%,"pb","pa",0 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("palettes",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("palettes",A%)
440 GOTO 9000
450 DEFPROCf(s$,k%):LOCAL w%,h%,x%,y%:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,s$ TO ,,,w%,h%
460 FOR y%=0 TO h%-1:FOR x%=0 TO w%-1:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,s$,x%,y%,x%+w%*y%+k%,0:NEXT:NEXT:ENDPROC
470 DEFPROCp(n$,s$):LOCAL w%,h%,m%,x%,y%,c%,t%,v%,S$:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,s$ TO ,,,w%,h%,m%
480 FOR y%=h%-1 TO 0 STEP -1:S$=n$+" "+STR$y%+":":FOR x%=0 TO w%-1
490 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+41,A%,s$,x%,y% TO ,,,,,c%,t%:S$=S$+" "+STR$~c%:IF t% THEN S$=S$+"t"+STR$~t%
500 IF m% THEN SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+43,A%,s$,x%,y% TO ,,,,,v%:S$=S$+"/"+STR$~v%
510 NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$:NEXT:ENDPROC
520 DEFFNo:LOCAL o%,n%:o%=A%!8:n%=A%!4:WHILE n%>1:o%+=A%!o%:n%-=1:ENDWHILE:=o%

40 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"one",0,5,3,12:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"two",0,3,2,28:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"Three",0,2,2,0

60 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+12,A%,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("save",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("save",A%)
70 SYS "OS_File",5,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO c%,,l%,,k%:PROCr("object",c%):PROCr("length",k%):PROCr("type",l%>>>8 AND &FFF)
80 DIM B% 4096:!B%=4096:B%!8=16:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+9,B%
90 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"four",0,2,2,12:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"two",0,6,1,12:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"one",0,1,1,28
100 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+10,B%,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("load",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("load",B%)
110 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+9,B%:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"four",0,2,2,12:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"two",0,6,1,12:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"Five",0,1,1,28
120 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+11,B%,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("merge",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("merge",B%)
130 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+11,B%,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("merge again",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("merge again",B%)
140 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+10,B%,"HostFS:$.SprMissing" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("missing",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("missing",B%)
150 F%=OPENOUT"HostFS:$.SprX":BPUT#F%,"not a sprite file at all":CLOSE#F%
160 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+10,B%,"HostFS:$.SprX" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("not sprites",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("not sprites",B%)
170 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"four",0,2,2,12
180 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+11,B%,"HostFS:$.SprX" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("merge not sprites",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("merge not",B%)
190 DIM C% 64:!C%=64:C%!8=16:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+9,C%
200 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+10,C%,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("too big",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("too big",C%)
210 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,C%,"x",0,1,1,12
220 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+11,C%,"HostFS:$.SprF" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("merge too big",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("merge too big",C%)
230 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+9,B%
240 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+12,B%,"HostFS:$.SprE" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("save empty",FNe(r0%,f%))
250 SYS "OS_File",5,"HostFS:$.SprE" TO c%:PROCr("empty object",c%)
260 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"y",0,1,1,12
270 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+12,B%,"HostFS:$.SprE" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("save one",FNe(r0%,f%))
280 SYS "OS_File",5,"HostFS:$.SprE" TO c%,,,,k%:PROCr("one object",c%):PROCr(" length",k%)
290 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+25,B%,"y"
300 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,B%,"z",0,1,1,12
310 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+11,B%,"HostFS:$.SprE" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("merge empty",FNe(r0%,f%)):PROCa("merge empty",B%)
320 SYS "OS_File",6,"HostFS:$.SprF":SYS "OS_File",6,"HostFS:$.SprX":SYS "OS_File",6,"HostFS:$.SprE"
330 GOTO 9000
340 DEFPROCf(s$,k%):LOCAL w%,h%,x%,y%:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,s$ TO ,,,w%,h%
350 FOR y%=0 TO h%-1:FOR x%=0 TO w%-1:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,s$,x%,y%,x%+w%*y%+k%,0:NEXT:NEXT:ENDPROC

case sprite_get
10 PROCmode(25)
20 DIM A% 16384:FOR i%=0 TO 16380 STEP 4:A%!i%=0:NEXT:!A%=16384:A%!8=16
30 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+9,A%
40 PROCmode(12)
50 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
60 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
70 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g1",0,0,0,63,7 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16",FNe(r0%,f%))
80 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g2",0,12,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16 wastage",FNe(r0%,f%))
90 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g3",0,30,10,12,4 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16 reversed",FNe(r0%,f%))
100 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g4",1,12,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16 palette",FNe(r0%,f%))
110 VDU 24,16;0;40;30;:GCOL 128+9
120 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g5",0,0,0,63,39 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16 window",FNe(r0%,f%))
130 VDU 26:ORIGIN 8,4
140 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g6",0,0,0,15,7 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16 origin",FNe(r0%,f%))
150 VDU 29,0;0;
160 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"g7",0,1270,1020,1290,1030 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get16 offscreen",FNe(r0%,f%))
170 MOVE 4,2:MOVE 20,10
180 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+14,A%,"g8",0 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get14",FNe(r0%,f%))
190 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+14,A%,"g8",0 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get14 replace",FNe(r0%,f%))
200 PROCz:PROCa("mode12",A%)
210 PROCmode(28)
220 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
230 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
240 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"h1",0,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get28",FNe(r0%,f%))
250 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"h2",1,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get28 palette",FNe(r0%,f%))
260 PROCmode(0)
270 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
280 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
290 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"h3",0,6,4,40,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get0",FNe(r0%,f%))
300 PROCmode(2)
310 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
320 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
330 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"h4",0,6,4,40,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("get2",FNe(r0%,f%))
340 PROCz:PROCa("numbered",A%)
350 PROCmode(25)
360 GOTO 9000
370 DEFPROCz:LOCAL o%,n%,w%,h%,b%,l%:o%=A%!8:n%=A%!4
380 WHILE n%>0:w%=4*(A%!(o%+16)+1):h%=A%!(o%+20)+1:b%=A%!(o%+24):l%=A%!(o%+28):PROCzr(o%+A%!(o%+32),w%,h%,b%,l%)
390 IF (A%!(o%+40) AND &FFFFFF00)=0 AND A%!(o%+36)<>A%!(o%+32) THEN PROCzr(o%+A%!(o%+36),w%,h%,b%,l%)
400 o%+=A%!o%:n%-=1:ENDWHILE:ENDPROC
410 DEFPROCzr(s%,w%,h%,b%,l%):LOCAL r%:FOR r%=1 TO h%:A%!s%=A%!s% AND NOT ((1<<b%)-1)
420 IF l%<31 THEN A%!(s%+w%-4)=A%!(s%+w%-4) AND ((2<<l%)-1)
430 s%+=w%:NEXT:ENDPROC

case sprite_get_selectors
10 PROCmode(25)
20 DIM A% 65536:FOR i%=0 TO 65532 STEP 4:A%!i%=0:NEXT:!A%=65536:A%!8=16
30 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+9,A%

240 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,"s3" TO ,,,w%,c%,k%,m%;f%:IF (f% AND 1)=0 THEN PROCr(" w",w%):PROCr(" m",m%)
250 S%!0=1:S%!4=320:S%!8=256:S%!12=1:S%!16=-1:S%!20=-1:PROCscreen(S%)
260 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
270 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
280 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"s4",0,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("sel1",FNe(r0%,f%))
290 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,"s4" TO ,,,w%,c%,k%,m%;f%:IF (f% AND 1)=0 THEN PROCr(" w",w%):PROCr(" m",m%)
300 S%!0=1:S%!4=320:S%!8=256:S%!12=0:S%!16=-1:S%!20=-1:PROCscreen(S%)
310 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
320 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
330 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"s5",0,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("sel0",FNe(r0%,f%))
340 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,"s5" TO ,,,w%,c%,k%,m%;f%:IF (f% AND 1)=0 THEN PROCr(" w",w%):PROCr(" m",m%)
350 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"s9",1,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("deep palette",FNe(r0%,f%))
360 S%!0=1:S%!4=320:S%!8=256:S%!12=4:S%!16=-1:S%!20=0:S%!24=&80:S%!28=-1:PROCscreen(S%)
370 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
380 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
390 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"s8",0,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("sel 4K",FNe(r0%,f%))
400 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+40,A%,"s8" TO ,,,w%,c%,k%,m%;f%:IF (f% AND 1)=0 THEN PROCr(" m",m%)
410 S%!0=1:S%!4=320:S%!8=256:S%!12=3:S%!16=-1:S%!20=0:S%!24=&80:S%!28=3:S%!32=255:S%!36=-1:PROCscreen(S%)
420 FOR i%=0 TO 15:GCOL i%:RECTANGLE FILL i%*8,0,7,40:NEXT
430 GCOL 3:MOVE 0,60:DRAW 200,100
440 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+16,A%,"s7",1,4,4,30,10 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("sel full",FNe(r0%,f%))
450 PROCa("selectors",A%)
460 PROCmode(25)

case sprite_output
10 PROCmode(25)

100 $N%="s":PROCsw("to s",256+60,A%,N%,V%):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
110 PROCvars("in s")
120 GCOL 128+3:CLG:GCOL 5:MOVE 0,0:DRAW 20,10:RECTANGLE FILL 10,10,8,6
130 VDU 4:PRINT TAB(0,0)"Hi";
140 PROCvars("drawn s")
150 PROCsw("back",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
160 PROCr(" saved",V%!0<>0):PROCvars("back")
170 PROCa("a",A%)
180 PROCsw("to s again",256+60,A%,N%,V%):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
190 PROCr(" gx",FNv(138)):PROCr(" gy",FNv(139)):PROCr(" tx",POS):PROCr(" ty",VPOS)
200 PROCsw("back again",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
210 PROCsw("no save area",256+60,A%,N%,0):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
220 PROCr(" gx",FNv(138)):PROCr(" gy",FNv(139))
230 PROCsw("back none",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
240 PROCsw("kernel area",256+60,A%,N%,1):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
250 PROCr(" gx",FNv(138)):PROCr(" gy",FNv(139))
260 PROCsw("back kernel",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
270 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+61,A%,"s",W% TO r0%;f%:PROCr("mask none",FNe(r0%,f%))
280 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+29,A%,"s"
290 PROCsw("to mask",256+61,A%,N%,W%):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
300 PROCvars("in mask")
310 GCOL 128+0:CLG:GCOL 1:MOVE 2,2:DRAW 30,20
320 PROCsw("mask back",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
330 PROCa("b",A%)
340 FOR i%=0 TO 1020 STEP 4:W%!i%=0:NEXT
350 $N%="t":PROCsw("to t",256+60,A%,N%,W%):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
360 PROCvars("in t")
370 GCOL 128+3:CLG:GCOL 5:MOVE 0,0:DRAW 10,4
380 PROCsw("t back",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
390 PROCa("c",A%)
400 MODE 25:PROCfont:VDU 23,1,0;0;0;0;
410 $N%="s":PROCsw("to s mode",256+60,A%,N%,V%):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
420 MODE 25:PROCfont:VDU 23,1,0;0;0;0;
430 PROCvars("after mode")
440 PROCsw("restore after mode",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
450 PROCvars("restored after mode")
460 GOTO 9000
470 DEFPROCq(n$,v%):LOCAL d%,z%:SYS "OS_ReadDynamicArea",3 TO d%,z%
480 IF v%>=A% AND v%<A%+!A% THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" A+"+STR$~(v%-A%):ENDPROC
490 IF v%>=V% AND v%<V%+1024 THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" V+"+STR$~(v%-V%):ENDPROC
500 IF v%>=W% AND v%<W%+1024 THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" W+"+STR$~(v%-W%):ENDPROC
510 IF v%=FNv(149) THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" screen":ENDPROC
520 IF z% AND v%>=d% AND v%<d%+(z% AND &7FFFFFFF) THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" S+"+STR$~(v%-d%):ENDPROC
530 BPUT#R%,n$+" "+STR$~v%:ENDPROC
540 DEFPROCvars(n$):LOCAL i%,S$:S$=n$+" m":FOR i%=0 TO 12:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNm(i%):NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$
550 S$=n$+" v":FOR i%=128 TO 147:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNv(i%):NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$
560 S$=n$+" w":FOR i%=150 TO 165:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNv(i%):NEXT:FOR i%=167 TO 170:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNv(i%):NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$
570 S$=n$+" x "+STR$~FNv(256)+" "+STR$~FNv(257):BPUT#R%,S$:PROCq(n$+" start",FNv(148)):ENDPROC
580 DEFPROCsw(n$,r0%,r1%,r2%,r3%):SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",r0%,r1%,r2%,r3% TO o0%,o1%,o2%,o3%;f%:PROCr(n$,FNe(o0%,f%))
590 PROCr(" r0",o0%):PROCq(" r1",o1%):PROCq(" r2",o2%):PROCq(" r3",o3%):ENDPROC

1000 GCOL 128+0:CLG:GCOL 1:MOVE 0,0:DRAW 16,8
1010 PROCsw(" back",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
1020 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+15,A%,"n",0,9,5,&B01680B5 TO r0%;f%:PROCr("n",FNe(r0%,f%))
1030 SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+29,A%,"n"
1040 FOR i%=0 TO 1020 STEP 4:V%!i%=0:NEXT
1050 $N%="n":PROCsw("wide mask",256+61,A%,N%,V%):s0%=o0%:s1%=o1%:s2%=o2%:s3%=o3%
1060 PROCvars("in wide mask")
1070 GCOL 128+0:CLG:GCOL 7:MOVE 0,0:DRAW 16,8
1080 PROCsw(" back",s0%,s1%,s2%,s3%)
1090 PROCa("masks",A%)
1100 GOTO 9000
1110 DEFPROCq(n$,v%):LOCAL d%,z%:SYS "OS_ReadDynamicArea",3 TO d%,z%
1120 IF v%>=A% AND v%<A%+!A% THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" A+"+STR$~(v%-A%):ENDPROC
1130 IF v%>=V% AND v%<V%+1024 THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" V+"+STR$~(v%-V%):ENDPROC
1140 IF v%>=W% AND v%<W%+1024 THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" W+"+STR$~(v%-W%):ENDPROC
1150 IF v%=FNv(149) THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" screen":ENDPROC
1160 IF z% AND v%>=d% AND v%<d%+(z% AND &7FFFFFFF) THEN BPUT#R%,n$+" S+"+STR$~(v%-d%):ENDPROC
1170 BPUT#R%,n$+" "+STR$~v%:ENDPROC
1180 DEFPROCvars(n$):LOCAL i%,S$:S$=n$+" m":FOR i%=0 TO 12:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNm(i%):NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$
1190 S$=n$+" v":FOR i%=128 TO 147:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNv(i%):NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$
1200 S$=n$+" w":FOR i%=150 TO 165:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNv(i%):NEXT:FOR i%=167 TO 170:S$=S$+" "+STR$~FNv(i%):NEXT:BPUT#R%,S$
1210 S$=n$+" x "+STR$~FNv(256)+" "+STR$~FNv(257):BPUT#R%,S$:PROCq(n$+" start",FNv(148)):ENDPROC
1220 DEFPROCsw(n$,r0%,r1%,r2%,r3%):SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",r0%,r1%,r2%,r3% TO o0%,o1%,o2%,o3%;f%:PROCr(n$,FNe(o0%,f%))
1230 PROCr(" r0",o0%):PROCq(" r1",o1%):PROCq(" r2",o2%):PROCq(" r3",o3%):ENDPROC
1240 DEFFNo:LOCAL o%,n%:o%=A%!8:n%=A%!4:WHILE n%>1:o%+=A%!o%:n%-=1:ENDWHILE:=o%

case colourtrans_matches_at_every_depth
10 DIM c%(23):s%=97:FOR i%=0 TO 23:s%=s% EOR (s%<<13):s%=s% EOR (s%>>>17):s%=s% EOR (s%<<5):c%(i%)=s% AND &FFFFFF00:NEXT

80 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,"g",x%,y%,200 OR (100<<8) OR (50<<16),0:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,"p8",x%,y%,x%+16*y%,0:NEXT:NEXT
90 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+38,A%,"g",&80000000:FOR y%=0 TO 15:FOR x%=0 TO 15:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+44,A%,"g",x%,y%,x%+16*y%:NEXT:NEXT
100 P%=C%:[OPT 0:EOR R0,R0,#&FF00:EOR R0,R0,#&FF0000:EOR R0,R0,#&FF000000:MOV PC,R14:]:D%!0=C%:D%!4=C%
110 PROCmode(8):SYS "ColourTrans_WritePalette",-1,-1,T%,0,0:SYS "ColourTrans_InvalidateCache"
120 SYS "ColourTrans_SelectTable",A%,"d32",-1,-1,H%:SYS "ColourTrans_SelectTable",A%,"d16",-1,-1,G%:SYS "ColourTrans_SelectTable",A%,"p8",-1,-1,B%
130 SYS "OS_SetColour",0,2:RECTANGLE FILL 0,200,1279,100
140 FOR Y%=0 TO 200 STEP 200:PROCs("d32",0,Y%,&5000,H%):PROCs("p8",80,Y%,&5000,B%):PROCs("p8",160,Y%,&1800,B%):PROCs("g",240,Y%,&5008,H%)
150 PROCs("d16",320,Y%,&5000,G%):PROCs("d32",400,Y%,&C800,H%):PROCs("g",480,Y%,8,H%):PROCs("d32",560,Y%,&80,D%):NEXT
160 PROCs("p8",640,0,&8000,0):PROCs("d32",720,0,0,0)
170 SYS "BlendTable_GenerateTable",0,-1,-1,-1,-1,80,X%:FOR i%=0 TO 15:PROCr("blend",X%?i%):NEXT:GOTO 9000
180 DEFPROCs(s$,x%,y%,f%,t%):SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+52,A%,s$,x%,y%,f%,S%,t% TO r%;f%:PROCr(s$,FNe(r%,f%)):ENDPROC

case sprite_blends_onto_256_colours

110 Q%!0=1:Q%!4=640:Q%!8=480:Q%!12=3:Q%!16=-1:Q%!20=0:Q%!24=&80:Q%!28=3:Q%!32=255:Q%!36=-1:PROCscreen(Q%):SYS "ColourTrans_WritePalette",-1,-1,W%,0,0:SYS "ColourTrans_InvalidateCache"

170 SYS "BlendTable_GenerateTable",0,-1,-1,-1,-1,80,X%:FOR i%=0 TO 65535 STEP 2053:PROCr("blend",X%?i%):NEXT:GOTO 9000
180 DEFPROCs(s$,x%,y%,f%,t%):SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+52,A%,s$,x%,y%,f%,S%,t% TO r%;f%:PROCr(s$,FNe(r%,f%)):ENDPROC

case sprite_blends_onto_sixteen_bit_colour
10 DIM Q% 64:Q%!0=1:Q%!4=640:Q%!8=480:Q%!12=4:Q%!16=-1:Q%!20=-1:PROCscreen(Q%)
20 DIM A% 65536:!A%=65536:A%!8=16:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+9,A%:DIM S% 16:S%!0=1:S%!4=1:S%!8=1:S%!12=1:DIM V% 16

40 FOR y%=380 TO 479:FOR x%=0 TO 319:B%!(y%*L%+x%*4)=((x%*37+y%*11) AND &FFFF) OR (((x%*13+y%*29) AND &FFFF)<<16):NEXT:NEXT

80 FOR y%=0 TO 15:FOR x%=0 TO 15:c%=(x%*16+7) OR ((y%*16+3)<<8) OR (((x%*y%) AND 255)<<16):e%=(x%*2) OR ((y%*2)<<5) OR (((x%+y%) AND 31)<<10)
90 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,"g",x%,y%,c%,0:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,"d32",x%,y%,c%,0:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,"d16",x%,y%,e%,0:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+42,A%,"h",x%,y%,e%,0
100 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+44,A%,"g",x%,y%,x%+16*y%:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",256+44,A%,"h",x%,y%,255-x%-16*y%:NEXT:NEXT
110 FOR Y%=16 TO 100 STEP 84:PROCs("g",16,Y%,&8):PROCs("g",64,Y%,&4008):PROCs("g",112,Y%,&8008):PROCs("g",160,Y%,&C008)
120 PROCs("d32",208,Y%,&4000):PROCs("d32",256,Y%,&8000):PROCs("d32",304,Y%,&C000):PROCs("d16",352,Y%,&4000)
130 PROCs("d16",400,Y%,&8000):PROCs("d16",448,Y%,&C000):PROCs("h",496,Y%,&8):PROCs("h",544,Y%,&8008):NEXT
140 GOTO 9000
150 DEFPROCs(s$,x%,y%,f%):SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",256+52,A%,s$,x%,y%,f%,S%,0 TO r%;f%:PROCr(s$,FNe(r%,f%)):ENDPROC

case sprite_blends_onto_32_bit_colour

case output_switched_to_a_sprite_invalidates_colourtrans_through_colourv
10 PROCmode(28):DIM c% 512,l% 4096,a% 4096
20 FOR s%=0 TO 2 STEP 2:FOR v%=0 TO 1:P%=c%+v%*256:[OPT s%
30 STMFD R13!,{R9-R11}:LDR R9,la:LDR R10,[R9]:CMP R10,#3600:ADDLT R10,R10,#20:STRLT R10,[R9]:ADDLT R9,R9,R10:ADDLT R9,R9,#4
40 MOVLT R11,#v%:STRLT R11,[R9],#4:STMLTIA R9,{R0,R3,R8}:LDMFD R13!,{R9-R11}:MOV PC,R14
50 .la EQUD l%:]:NEXT:NEXT
60 !a%=4096:a%!4=0:a%!8=16:a%!12=16:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&10F,a%,"t",1,8,8,0:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&11D,a%,"t"
70 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&118,a%,"t" TO ,,p%
80 FOR k%=0 TO 1:SYS "OS_Claim",&22,c%,0:SYS "OS_Claim",&1F,c%+256,0:!l%=-20
90 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&23C+k%,a%,p%,0 TO r0%,r1%,r2%,r3%:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",r0%,r1%,r2%,r3%
100 SYS "OS_Release",&22,c%,0:SYS "OS_Release",&1F,c%+256,0
110 FOR j%=0 TO !l% STEP 20:IF l%!(4+j%)=0 THEN PROCr("colourv",l%!(16+j%)) ELSE PROCr("spritev",l%!(8+j%)):PROCr(" r3",l%!(12+j%))
120 NEXT:NEXT
130 GOTO 9000

case sprite_transformed_hands_draw_its_corners
10 PROCmode(28):DIM c% 512,l% 4096,a% 4096,m% 32,g% 32,t% 256,u% 256,e% 64
20 e%!0=&ABC:$(e%+4)="Refused":FOR i%=0 TO 255:t%?i%=255-i%:u%?i%=i%:NEXT
30 FOR s%=0 TO 2 STEP 2:P%=c%:[OPT s%
40 STMFD R13!,{R0-R12,R14}:LDR R9,la:LDR R10,[R9]:CMP R10,#3600:BGE dn:ADD R10,R10,#80:STR R10,[R9]:ADD R9,R9,R10:ADD R9,R9,#4:STMIA R9!,{R0-R8}
50 CMP R8,#10:CMPNE R8,#0:BNE dn:MOV R11,#10
60 .cp LDR R12,[R0],#4:STR R12,[R9],#4:SUBS R11,R11,#1:BNE cp
70 .dn LDR R11,md:CMP R11,#0:BEQ ps:LDR R8,[R13,#32]:CMP R8,#10:BNE ps:CMP R11,#2:BNE nm
80 LDR R0,[R13]:MOV R1,#300*256:STR R1,[R0,#4]:STR R1,[R0,#8]:MOV R2,#332*256:STR R2,[R0,#16]:STR R1,[R0,#20]:STR R1,[R0,#28]:STR R2,[R0,#32]
90 .nm CMP R11,#3:BEQ er:LDMFD R13!,{R0-R12,R14}:LDMFD R13!,{PC}
100 .er LDMFD R13!,{R0-R12,R14}:LDR R0,eb:MSR CPSR_f,#&10000000:LDMFD R13!,{PC}
110 .ps LDMFD R13!,{R0-R12,R14}:MOV PC,R14
120 .la EQUD l%:.md EQUD 0:.eb EQUD e%:]:NEXT
130 !a%=4096:a%!4=0:a%!8=16:a%!12=16:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&10F,a%,"s",0,8,8,28:SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&11D,a%,"s"
140 SYS "OS_SpriteOp",&118,a%,"s" TO ,,p%:i%=p%+p%!32:FOR j%=0 TO 63:i%?j%=255-j%:NEXT
150 GCOL 0,128+12
160 PROCm(&20000,0,0,&20000,100,100):PROCt("matrix",&238,0,0,0,m%,0,0)
170 PROCt("moved",&238,0,0,0,m%,0,2)
180 PROCt("refused",&238,0,0,0,m%,0,3)
190 PROCm(&20000,0,0,&20000,&7FFFFF,100):PROCt("overflow",&238,0,0,0,m%,0,0)
200 g%!0=-4:g%!4=-4:g%!8=20:g%!12=20:PROCm(&20000,0,0,&20000,200,100):PROCt("held",&238,2,g%,0,m%,0,0)
210 g%!0=6:g%!4=7:g%!8=2:g%!12=1:PROCm(&20000,0,0,&20000,400,100):PROCt("backward",&238,2,g%,0,m%,0,0)
220 PROCm(&10000,0,0,&10000,100,300):PROCt("table",&238,0,0,0,m%,t%,0)
230 PROCm(&10000,0,0,&10000,160,300):PROCt("identity",&238,0,0,8,m%,u%,0)
240 PROCt("unmoved",&238,0,0,0,0,0,0)
250 PROCm(&18000,&8000,-&8000,&18000,500,300):PROCt("mask",&237,0,0,0,m%,0,0)
260 SYS "XOS_CLI","RMKill Draw" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("kill",FNe(r0%,f%))
270 PROCm(&20000,0,0,&20000,500,100):PROCt("drawless",&238,0,0,0,m%,0,0)
280 SYS "XOS_CLI","RMReInit Draw" TO r0%;f%:PROCr("reinit",FNe(r0%,f%))
290 PROCt("again",&238,0,0,0,m%,0,0)
300 GOTO 9000
310 DEFPROCm(a%,b%,c%,d%,x%,y%):m%!0=a%:m%!4=b%:m%!8=c%:m%!12=d%:m%!16=x%*256:m%!20=y%*256:ENDPROC
320 DEFPROCt(n$,o%,f%,r%,x%,q%,w%,k%):LOCAL r0%,g%,j%,i%,v%
330 !md=k%:!l%=-80:SYS "OS_Claim",&20,c%,0:SYS "XOS_SpriteOp",o%,a%,p%,f%,r%,x%,q%,w% TO r0%;g%:SYS "OS_Release",&20,c%,0:!md=0
340 PROCr(n$,FNe(r0%,g%)):IF !l%<0 THEN ENDPROC
350 FOR j%=0 TO !l% STEP 80:FOR i%=1 TO 8:v%=l%!(4+j%+4*i%):IF v%=m% THEN v%=-2 ELSE IF v%=t% OR v%=u% THEN v%=-3
360 PROCr(" r"+STR$i%,v%):NEXT:IF l%!(36+j%)=10 THEN FOR i%=0 TO 9:PROCr(" w"+STR$i%,l%!(40+j%+4*i%)):NEXT
370 NEXT:ENDPROC
